This is\n     5\tthe hook that a DQN-based controller should implement.\n     6\t\"\"\"\n     7\tfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\timport numpy as np\n    10\t\n    11\timport numpy as np\n    12\timport math\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import global configuration\n    15\timport sys\n    16\timport os\n    17\ttry:\n    18\t    sys.path.insert(0, @abstractmethod\n    31\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    32\t        \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\n    33\t\n    34\t 38\t\n    39\tclass StaticController(ParamController):\n    40\t    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\n    41\t        self.params = dict(params)\n    42\t\n    43\t    def reset(self,   48\t\n    49\t\n    50\tclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    51\t    \"\"\"A simple heuristic that adjusts DE/JADE parameters based on progress.\n    52\t\n    53\t    Early iterations favor exploration (large F/CR, broad p-best sampling);\n    54\t    later iterations favor exploitation (smaller F/CR, narrower p-best).\n    55\t    \"\"\"\n    56\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    57\t                 p_best_start=0.5, p_best_end=0.1, decay_iters=100):\n    58\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    59\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    60\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    61\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    62\t        self.p_best_start = p_best_start\n    63\t        self.p_best_end = p_best_end\n    64\t        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n    65\t\n    66\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    67\t        self.it = 0\n    68\t\n    69\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    70\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n    71\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n    72\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n    73\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n    74\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n    75\t        # p-best shrinks over time to focus search around elite individuals\n    76\t        p_best = self.p_best_start * (1 - t) + self.p_best_end * t\n    77\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'p_best': float(p_best)}\n    78\t\n    79\t\n    80\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    81\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    82\t\n    83\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    84\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    85\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    86\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    87\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    88\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n    89\t      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n    90\t      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    91\t    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n    92\t      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    93\t    \"\"\"\n    94\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    95\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    96\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    97\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    98\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    99\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n   100\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n   101\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n   102\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n   103\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n   104\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n   105\t\n   106\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n   107\t        self.it = 0\n   108\t        self.last_gbest = None\n   109\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   110\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   111\t\n   112\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   113\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   114\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   115\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   116\t\n   117\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   118\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   119\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   120\t\n   121\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   122\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   123\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   124\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   125\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   126\t            else:\n   127\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   128\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   129\t\n   130\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n   131\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   132\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   133\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   134\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   135\t\n   136\t        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n   137\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   138\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   139\t        else:\n   140\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   143\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   144\t        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n   145\t        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n   146\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   147\t        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n   148\t\n   149\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   150\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   151\t        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n   152\t        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n   153\t            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n   154\t\n   155\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n   156\t                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

  48\t\n    49\t\n    50\tclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    51\t    \"\"\"A simple heuristic that adjusts DE/JADE parameters based on progress.\n    52\t\n    53\t    Early iterations favor exploration (large F/CR, broad p-best sampling);\n    54\t    later iterations favor exploitation (smaller F/CR, narrower p-best).\n    55\t    \"\"\"\n    56\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    57\t                 p_best_start=0.5, p_best_end=0.1, decay_iters=100):\n    58\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    59\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    60\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    61\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    62\t        self.p_best_start = p_best_start\n    63\t        self.p_best_end = p_best_end\n    64\t        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n    65\t\n    66\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    67\t        self.it = 0\n    68\t\n    69\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    70\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n    71\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n    72\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n    73\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n    74\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n    75\t        # p-best shrinks over time to focus search around elite individuals\n    76\t        p_best = self.p_best_start * (1 - t) + self.p_best_end * t\n    77\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'p_best': float(p_best)}\n    78\t\n    79\t\n    80\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    81\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    82\t\n    83\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    84\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    85\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    86\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    87\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    88\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n    89\t      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n    90\t      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    91\t    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n    92\t      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    93\t    \"\"\"\n    94\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    95\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    96\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    97\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    98\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    99\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n   100\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n   101\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n   102\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n   103\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n   104\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n   105\t\n   106\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n   107\t        self.it = 0\n   108\t        self.last_gbest = None\n   109\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   110\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   111\t\n   112\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   113\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   114\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   115\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   116\t\n   117\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   118\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   119\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   120\t\n   121\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   122\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   123\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   124\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   125\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   126\t            else:\n   127\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   128\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   129\t\n   130\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n   131\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   132\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   133\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   134\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   135\t\n   136\t        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n   137\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   138\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   139\t        else:\n   140\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   143\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   144\t        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n   145\t        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n   146\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   147\t        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n   148\t\n   149\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   150\t        # It is controlled by an optional `controller` object\n+implementing the ParamController interface, which can dynamically bias the F and\n+CR memory slots used by the optimizer.\n+\"\"\"\n+\n+from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n+import numpy as np\n+import os\n+import sys\n+\n+# Import SEED from config if available\n+_config_seed = None\n+sys.path.insert(0, style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n+        if x.ndim == 1:\n+            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n+        result = problem.func(x)\n+        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n+    else:\n+        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n+        return float(problem(x))\n+\n+\n+class DEOptimizer:\n+    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n+    \n+    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n+                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n+                 swarm_size: int = None):\n+        self.dim = int(dim)\n+        if lower_bounds is not None:\n+            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n+            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n+                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n+        else:\n+            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n+        \n+        if upper_bounds is not None:\n+            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n+            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n+                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n+        else:\n+            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n+        \n+        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n+        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n+        self.controller = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n+        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n+\n+    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n+        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n+        selected = []\n+        for _ in range(tournsize):\n+            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n+            selected.append(idx)\n+        best_idx = selected[0]\n+        for idx in selected[1:]:\n+            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n+                best_idx = idx\n+        return best_idx\n+\n+    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n+        if self.controller is not None:\n+            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': \n+        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n+        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n+\n+        # SHADE / JADE style control-parameter memory\n+        H = 20\n+        mu_F = np.full(H, 0.5)\n+        mu_CR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n+        memory_index = 0\n+        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n+\n+        # Initialize population - quasi-random (Latin if evals >= self.max_evals:\n+                break\n+            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n+            evals += 1\"\n    }\n    </json>",

    "content": "<json>\n{\n    \"response\": \"Implemented several performance improvements in the JADE/SHADE DE optimizer and added a new progress-aware controller.\\n\\nChanges made to ec_algorithm.py:\\n1. Added L-SHADE style linear population size reduction, shrinking the population from its initial size down to 4 as evaluations are consumed. This focuses computational budget on promising individuals late in the run.\\n2. Added dynamic p-best ratio scheduling, annealing from the configured initial p-best toward a smaller exploitation-focused p-best over the evaluation budget, with the controller able to override it.\\n3. Added a safe lower bound (0.05) on the sampled F parameter, preventing excessively small mutation steps that stagnate the search.\\n\\nChanges made to param_controller.py:\\n1. Added a new ImprovedDEController that schedules F/CR/p_best based on evaluation progress, detects stagnation, and temporarily boosts F/CR/p_best to recover diversity when no improvement is made.\\n2. Correctly initializes controller state in __init__ so it can be used directly without requiring reset() first.\\n\\nThe optimizer remains fully compatible with the existing DQN controller interface and scoring/evaluation pipeline, and was tested locally for correctness and evaluation-count compliance.\"\n}\n</json>",
    "text": "<json>\n{\n    \"response\": \"Implemented several performance improvements in the JADE/SHADE DE optimizer and added a new progress-aware controller.\\n\\nChanges made to ec_algorithm.py:\\n1. Added L-SHADE style linear population size reduction, shrinking the population from its initial size down to 4 as evaluations are consumed. This focuses computational budget on promising individuals late in the run.\\n2. Added dynamic p-best ratio scheduling, annealing from the configured initial p-best toward a smaller exploitation-focused p-best over the evaluation budget, with the controller able to override it.\\n3. Added a safe lower bound (0.05) on the sampled F parameter, preventing excessively small mutation steps that stagnate the search.\\n\\nChanges made to param_controller.py:\\n1. Added a new ImprovedDEController that schedules F/CR/p_best based on evaluation progress, detects stagnation, and temporarily boosts F/CR/p_best to recover diversity when no improvement is made.\\n2. Correctly initializes controller state in __init__ so it can be used directly without requiring reset() first.\\n\\nThe optimizer remains fully compatible with the existing DQN controller interface and scoring/evaluation pipeline, and was tested locally for correctness and evaluation-count compliance.\"\n}\n</json>"
